# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-13T11:37:02+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Fri, 13 Nov 2026 07:44:00 -0300 — Outlook, Teams e Copilot, da Microsoft, ainda estão fora do ar
  O Outlook, o Teams e o Copilot, da Microsoft, continuavam fora do ar nesta sexta-feira (13), quando uma das piores falhas da empresa chega a mais de 12 horas, prejudicando as comunicações em todo o mundo. A Microsoft atribuiu a interrupção a problemas nos servidores e afirmou não ter conhecimento de nenhuma atividade maliciosa. Em sua última atualização hoje, a empresa informou que observa sinais de estabilização após implementar correções de forma mais ampla para alcançar uma retomada sustentável dos serviços. Notícias relacionadas: Microsoft 365 e Outlook saem do ar para milhares de usuários. Apagão cibernético afetou 8,5 milhões de computadores da Microsoft. A interrupção afetou órgãos governamentais na Austrália, no Japão e nos Estados Unidos, além de causar transtornos em corretoras,
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/internacional/noticia/2026-11/outlook-teams-e-copilot-da-microsoft-ainda-estao-fora-do-ar
- Thu, 12 Nov 2026 21:35:00 -0300 — STF encerra processo sobre perdas inflacionárias em planos econômicos
  O Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF) confirmou, nesta quinta-feira (12), a validade da decisão que homologou um acordo para encerrar os processos judiciais sobre perdas financeiras de poupadores provocadas por planos econômicos das décadas de 1980 e 1990. O plenário rejeitou três recursos apresentados por entidades sem legitimidade jurídica para contestar a decisão da Corte. Com isso, o Supremo determinou o encerramento definitivo do caso e manteve até junho de 2027 o prazo para adesão ao acordo. Em março de 2018, o STF homologou o acordo final firmado entre a Advocacia-Geral da União (AGU), representantes de bancos e associações de defesa do consumidor para encerrar as ações judiciais sobre o tema. O acordo prevê a correção de aplicações na poupança durante a entrada em vigor dos planos econô
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/justica/noticia/2026-11/stf-encerra-processo-sobre-perdas-inflacionarias-em-planos-economicos
- Thu, 12 Nov 2026 21:26:00 -0300 — PGR é contra revisar condenação de Jair Bolsonaro
  O procurador-geral da República, Paulo Gonet, enviou nesta quinta-feira (12) ao Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF) parecer contra o pedido de revisão criminal protocolado pela defesa do ex-presidente Jair Bolsonaro. Na petição, protocolada no mês passado no Supremo, a defesa de Bolsonaro pretende derrubar a decisão da Primeira Turma que condenou o ex-presidente a 27 anos e três meses de prisão no processo que julgou a tentativa de golpe de estado no Brasil . Notícias relacionadas: Justiça Federal rejeita ação de improbidade contra Bolsonaro. Na manifestação, Gonet disse que as provas utilizadas pelo STF demonstraram a estabilidade de uma organização voltada a um fim bem definido. “As dúvidas que a petição inicial, apoiada no parecer técnico, suscita sobre a rastreabilidade de procedimentos téc
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/justica/noticia/2026-11/pgr-e-contra-revisar-condenacao-de-jair-bolsonaro
- Thu, 12 Nov 2026 20:06:00 -0300 — Caixa Seguridade informa ataque cibernético e vazamento de dados
  A Caixa Seguridade informou, nesta quinta-feira (12), em comunicado ao mercado, que sua subsidiária Caixa Vida e Previdência identificou um ataque cibernético ocorrido em 6 de novembro, que resultou no vazamento de dados dos 7,6 milhões de clientes ativos da companhia. Segundo a empresa, foram copiados clandestinamente CPFs, endereços, telefones, e-mails e datas de nascimento. Dados de ex-clientes também foram vazados. “Com base na apuração preliminar, não há indícios de comprometimento de dados financeiros ou de autenticação, tais como valores de apólices, saldos de previdência, dados bancários ou senhas”, informou a Caixa Seguridade no comunicado. A companhia informou ainda que os titulares dos dados afetados estão sendo comunicados e recebendo recomendações de proteção diante do risco d
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/economia/noticia/2026-11/caixa-seguridade-informa-ataque-cibernetico-e-vazamento-de-dados

- undated — CHIME, the Copernicus hyperspectral mission enabling operational global imaging spectroscopy
  CHIME, the Copernicus hyperspectral mission enabling operational global imaging spectroscopy | Thales Alenia Space Skip to main content Next MTG-I2 in the starti... The Copernicus Hyperspectral Imaging Mission for the Environment (CHIME) is advancing its development to be ready for launch. CHIME will provide routine hyperspectral observations from space across the visible to shortwave infrared spectral range (400-2500 nm), supporting enhanced environmental services in areas such as food security, sustainable agriculture, biodiversity monitoring, soil characterisation, mineral resources and water quality. With a swath of approximately 130 km and a spatial sampling distance of 30 m, CHIME will complement Copernicus Sentinel-2 and extend Europe's Earth observation capabilities through operational imaging spectroscopy. - Wed, 11 Nov 2026 22:09:35 +0000 — The Ice Giants' Magnetic Fields are Very Strange Compared to Earth's
  Uranus and Neptune, the Solar System's ice giants, have interiors unlike the other planets. As a result, they also have magnetic fields unlike the others. They're messy and tilted, and Voyager 2, the only spacecraft to even flyby them, showed us how messy they are. New research uses models to try to understand what's going on with the pair of planets and their weird fields, and found that it's likely they change on very short timescales. They could already be much different than when Voyager 2 visited in the late 1980s.

  La Nación / Mejoras en el Incan: obras del futuro Centro de Atención Ambulatoria registra 90 % de avance Las obras que se desarrollan en el marco de la remodelación, refacción y ampliación del Instituto Nacional del Cáncer ( Incan ) ingresan en su etapa decisiva , informó el Ministerio de Obras Públicas y Comunicaciones (MOPC). Los trabajos para el futuro Centro de Atención Ambulatoria del Incan registran un avance cercano al 90 % . En paralelo, las obras del Parque Sanitario superan el 80 % de ejecución , detalló la institución acerca del plan de renovación integral del centro asistencial especializado. Las nuevas instalaciones brindarán espacios más modernos, seguros y funcionales a los miles de pacientes que acuden a diario al principal centro público de referencia oncológica del país para acceder a diagnósticos, tratamientos y seguimiento especializado. El primer hilo que revisamos en el foro de Samsung en Estados Unidos es del 8 de octubre. Android Authority reunió los casos el 5 de noviembre y le pidió una explicación a la empresa. Si tu teléfono va bien y todavía no instalaste One UI 9, lo prudente es esperar una vers
  https://revistamercado.do/technology/devices/one-ui-9-wifi-samsung-galaxy-fallo/

- Fri, 13 Nov 2026 07:44:00 -0300 — Outlook, Teams e Copilot, da Microsoft, ainda estão fora do ar
  O Outlook, o Teams e o Copilot, da Microsoft, continuavam fora do ar nesta sexta-feira (13), quando uma das piores falhas da empresa chega a mais de 12 horas, prejudicando as comunicações em todo o mundo. A Microsoft atribuiu a interrupção a problemas nos servidores e afirmou não ter conhecimento de nenhuma atividade maliciosa. Em sua última atualização hoje, a empresa informou que observa sinais de estabilização após implementar correções de forma mais ampla para alcançar uma retomada sustentável dos serviços. Notícias relacionadas: Microsoft 365 e Outlook saem do ar para milhares de usuários. Apagão cibernético afetou 8,5 milhões de computadores da Microsoft. A interrupção afetou órgãos governamentais na Austrália, no Japão e nos Estados Unidos, além de causar transtornos em corretoras,
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/internacional/noticia/2026-11/outlook-teams-e-copilot-da-microsoft-ainda-estao-fora-do-ar
- Thu, 12 Nov 2026 21:35:00 -0300 — STF encerra processo sobre perdas inflacionárias em planos econômicos
  O Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF) confirmou, nesta quinta-feira (12), a validade da decisão que homologou um acordo para encerrar os processos judiciais sobre perdas financeiras de poupadores provocadas por planos econômicos das décadas de 1980 e 1990. O plenário rejeitou três recursos apresentados por entidades sem legitimidade jurídica para contestar a decisão da Corte. Com isso, o Supremo determinou o encerramento definitivo do caso e manteve até junho de 2027 o prazo para adesão ao acordo. Em março de 2018, o STF homologou o acordo final firmado entre a Advocacia-Geral da União (AGU), representantes de bancos e associações de defesa do consumidor para encerrar as ações judiciais sobre o tema.   https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/justica/noticia/2026-11/stf-encerra-processo-sobre-perdas-inflacionarias-em-planos-economicos
- Thu, 12 Nov 2026 21:26:00 -0300 — PGR é contra revisar condenação de Jair Bolsonaro
  O procurador-geral da República, Paulo Gonet, enviou nesta quinta-feira (12) ao Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF) parecer contra o pedido de revisão criminal protocolado pela defesa do ex-presidente Jair Bolsonaro. Na petição, protocolada no mês passado no Supremo, a defesa de Bolsonaro pretende derrubar a decisão da Primeira Turma que condenou o ex-presidente a 27 anos e três meses de prisão no processo que julgou a tentativa de golpe de estado no Brasil .   https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/justica/noticia/2026-11/pgr-e-contra-revisar-condenacao-de-jair-bolsonaro
- Thu, 12 Nov 2026 20:06:00 -0300 — Caixa Seguridade informa ataque cibernético e vazamento de dados
  A Caixa Seguridade informou, nesta quinta-feira (12), em comunicado ao mercado, que sua subsidiária Caixa Vida e Previdência identificou um ataque cibernético ocorrido em 6 de novembro, que resultou no vazamento de dados dos 7,6 milhões de clientes ativos da companhia. Segundo a empresa, foram copiados clandestinamente CPFs, endereços, telefones, e-mails e datas de nascimento. Dados de ex-clientes também foram vazados. “Com base na apuração preliminar, não há indícios de comprometimento de dados financeiros ou de autenticação, tais como valores de apólices, saldos de previdência, dados bancários ou senhas”, informou a Caixa Seguridade no comunicado. - Fri, 13 Nov 2026 05:00:00 GMT — Amendment of Class C Airspace; Laughlin Air Force Base, Del Rio, TX
  This action makes administrative amendments to the Laughlin AFB, TX, Class C airspace legal description. Specifically, the action updates the geographic coordinates of the airport reference point (ARP) to match the FAA&#39;s National Airspace System Resources (NASR) database information. Additionally, this action corrects the header format to conform with current FAA policy. Lastly, this action updates the outdated term &quot;Airport/Facility Directory&quot; to &quot;Chart Supplement.&quot; This action does not change the lateral boundaries, altitudes, or operating requirements of the Class C airspace area.

- undated — Gama Raises €15M Seed to Boost Europe’s Solar Array Supply Chain
  Gama Raises €15M Seed to Boost Europe’s Solar Array Supply Chain Skip to content Europe continues to stack the building blocks for a more robust space economy.&nbsp; Gama, a French startup developing ultra-light deployable solar arrays, announced a €15M seed round yesterday to boost Europe’s solar array supply chain. The round included funds from a mix of European and US VCs, including Transition Ventures, Planet A, Draper Associates, and Manta Ray. The funds will be used to develop a high-volume production facility outside of Paris, France, which will be capable of producing 100 MW of solar arrays annually. The first units—called Wings—are expected to start rolling off the line next year. Gama also plans to hire ~60 employees in engineering roles across mechanical, electrical, and software. The tech: Gama was initially founded to develop solar-sail technology—which uses the Sun’s energy
  https://payloadspace.com/gama-raises-e15m-seed-to-boost-europes-solar-array-supply-chain/
- undated — Exclusive: Spaceium Opens License for Refueling Hardware
  Exclusive: Spaceium Opens License for Refueling Hardware Skip to content Spaceium’s goal is to launch gas stations into orbit. The first step? Making sure spacecraft builders have access to the pump. Today, the company is opening up access to its OpenPort refueling tech, allowing any company to build and license the hardware for $1—a low price because Spaceium plans to make its money by selling fuel to spacecraft. “We are not a port business,” Spaceium cofounder and CEO Ashi Dissanayake told Payload. “We are not here to sell a refueling interface and make money. We want to develop this whole space economy so that any spacecraft can get filled.” Gas up: When a satellite runs out of fuel in orbit, its useful life is over. Many compare that business model to throwing out a car every time the gas light comes on.&nbsp; Companies such as Spaceium are hoping to change that calculation. Being ab
  https://payloadspace.com/exclusive-spaceium-opens-license-for-refueling-hardware/
- undated — NeuralSat Raises €2.9M to Build Orbital Data Relay Network
  NeuralSat Raises €2.9M to Build Orbital Data Relay Network Skip to content The Autobahn is coming for in-space data traffic. German startup NeuralSat announced a €2.9M ($3.2M) pre-seed funding round today to develop a 72-sat in-space data relay and processing network, aimed at speeding up the flow of data to and from space. Capnamic Ventures and Bayern Kapital led the round, which included additional participation from ZOHO.VC, Robin Capital, KIEMIS Holding, and Mission E Capital. NeuralSat is also a member of ESA’s Business Incubation Center (ESA BIC) and the NVIDIA Inception Program. Sharing is caring: Unlike other orbital compute startups—namely, those pitching orbital data centers (ODCs) as a replacement for terrestrial ones—NeuralSat is focusing primarily on the data being gathered and distributed in space today.   Senate Appropriations CJS Subcommittee Proposes $24.9 Billion for NASA in FY2027 &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com The Senate Appropriations Committee released five more FY2027 bills this evening, including the Commerce-Justice-Science bill that funds NASA. The CJS subcommittee is proposing $24.874 billion for NASA, slightly more than the $24.438 billion approved by Congress for FY2026. By contrast, the Trump Administration requested $18.8 billion in FY2027. NASA implemented a major reorganization earlier this year, merging and/or rearranging the mission directorates and budget structure. The subcommittee&#8217;s report explains that the changes happened too late and lacked sufficient detail for them to be incorporated in this bill, however.   https://spacepolicyonline.com/news/senate-appropriations-cjs-subcommittee-proposes-24-9-billion-for-nasa-in-fy2027/
- undated — NASA Looking for New Ways to Return Perseverance Samples From Mars
  NASA Looking for New Ways to Return Perseverance Samples From Mars &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com In January, NASA will begin assessing new strategies to return the samples now being collected by the Mars Perseverance rover to Earth. The agency ended an earlier Mars Sample Return mission last year because of cost concerns. At a virtual NASA Town Hall meeting for the planetary science community today, Program Executive for Mars Exploration George Tahu said the Perseverance Sample Retrieval Architecture (PSRA) strategy team will kick off in January and spend a year looking at alternative ways to get the samples back to Earth. Perseverance landed on Mars in 2021 and has been collecting carefully selected samples from Jezero Crater ever since.   https://spacepolicyonline.com/news/nasa-looking-for-new-ways-to-return-perseverance-samples-from-mars/

  NASA Shares Moon Base Progress, Contract Awards &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com NASA provided another update on the Moon Base project this weekend in conjunction with the MAX POWER airshow at Kennedy Space Center. Billions of dollars were awarded or soon will be for cargo landers, a nuclear-powered lunar rover, launch and transportation, as well as architecture and infrastructure capabilities. Although the agency is full steam ahead on Moon Base plans, the three CLPS missions expected to launch this year have all slipped to 2027 and hopes for another 10 in 2027 will wait until 2028.   Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. March 16, 2026 – Ottawa – National Defence / Canadian Armed Forces Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. Today in Ottawa, the Honourable David J. McGuinty, Minister of National Defence, announced an historic $200 million investment in core infrastructure for a Canadian-owned spaceport, along with other major new space-related capabilities and initiatives.   Senior is not a skill level: Rethinking startup hiring - SpaceQ Media Inc. Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content Senior is not a skill level. Credit: SpaceQ In my last column , I offered advice on defining job descriptions around what you need, rather than picking a title out of The Book. This column is about how you describe the person you want to fill that new role. Founders describing the person they wish they could hire almost always reach for the same word. Senior. They want someone senior in engineering, senior in sales, senior in operations. They want someone with real experience, someone who’s done this before, someone they can hand a hard problem to and trust it will get handled. It’s a reasonable thing to want. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht
  https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/06/30/defensie-bouwt-aan-space-command

#### Agencia Espacial Española (space_agency, es, newsroom)

- undated — Agencia de la Unión Europea para el Programa Espacial (EUSPA)
  Agencia de la Unión Europea para el Programa Espacial (EUSPA) Agencia de la Unión Europea para el Programa Espacial (EUSPA) Agencia Espacial Española > Agencia de la Unión Europea para el Programa Espacial (EUSPA) La Agencia de la Uni&oacute;n Europea para el Programa Espacial (EUSPA) , instituida mediante el Reglamento (UE) 2021/696, es el organismo fundamental encargado de la implementaci&oacute;n del Programa Espacial de la UE. Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . Entre sus competencias clave se encuentran asegurar la acreditaci&oacute;n de seguridad para todas las acciones
  https://www.aee.gob.es/Actividades/EUSPA.html
- undated — Relaciones internacionales y bilaterales
  Relaciones internacionales y bilaterales Relaciones internacionales y bilaterales Agencia Espacial Española > Relaciones internacionales y bilaterales La Agencia Espacial Espa&ntilde;ola (AEE) constituye la voz &uacute;nica y la representaci&oacute;n oficial de Espa&ntilde;a en el &aacute;mbito espacial internacional. La AEE tiene el mandato de unificar la postura nacional, aportando coherencia, coordinaci&oacute;n y una visi&oacute;n estrat&eacute;gica integral a la participaci&oacute;n de Espa&ntilde;a en el exterior. Esta labor asegura que los intereses cient&iacute;ficos, industriales y gubernamentales espa&ntilde;oles se defiendan de manera un&iacute;voca, tanto en los grandes foros multilaterales como en la relaci&oacute;n directa con otras potencias espaciales. #### MEDIA INAF (science, it, newsroom)

- undated — Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare
  Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare &#8211; MEDIA INAF Un drone sorvola un lago prosciugato nel deserto della California, eseguendo una serie di manovre che ricordano la discesa di un veicolo spaziale verso la superficie lunare. Non è una coincidenza: il volo fa parte di un esperimento della Nasa per rendere più sicuri e precisi i futuri allunaggi. Ma il drone sulla Luna non ci andrà mai: senza atmosfera, le sue eliche non potrebbero farlo volare. A interessare la Nasa è il sistema che trasporta, una tecnologia progettata per riconoscere i pericoli durante la discesa e aiutare i veicoli spaziali a scegliere autonomamente dove atterrare. Si chiama Splice , acronimo di Safe and Precise Landing &#8211; Integrated Capabilities Evolution , ed è un insieme di tecnologie di guida, navigazione e controllo sviluppate dall’agenzia spaziale statunitense per le future missioni sulla Luna,
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/11/10/un-drone-nel-deserto-per-imparare-ad-atterrare-sulla-luna/
- undated — Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna
  Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna &#8211; MEDIA INAF Chiunque abbia provato a usare uno smartphone lasciato per ore al freddo sa che batterie e basse temperature non vanno particolarmente d’accordo. Sulla Luna, però, il problema è decisamente più serio: al polo sud, durante la notte, le temperature possono scendere fino a circa 200 gradi sotto zero, mentre nelle zone illuminate dal Sole la superficie può superare i 100 gradi Celsius. E se in futuro astronauti, rover e strumenti scientifici dovranno trascorrere sempre più tempo da quelle parti, qualcosa dovrà pur tenerli accesi. È il problema al centro di SpearS, acronimo di Solid Power for Extreme Environment Adaptability and Resilience in Space , un progetto della Nasa appena partito sotto la guida di Donald Dornbusch , del Glenn Research Center di Cleveland, per sviluppare batterie allo stato solido capaci di resistere alle
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/10/19/come-si-tiene-accesa-una-batteria-sulla-luna/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

- undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/

- undated — Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick
  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha
  https://www.blick.ch/politik/vertrag-unterzeichnet-vbs-erhaelt-satellitenbilder-aus-frankreich/lv5p0cg

Eine Finanzierung kann verschiedene Formen annehmen, darunter direkte Zuschüsse für Forschungsprojekte, Unterstützung für die Entwicklung neuer Technologien und Förderung von Kooperationen zwischen Hochschulen und der Industrie. Bojový pilot Aleš Svoboda uspěl v náročném výběrovém řízení Evropské kosmické agentury, kam se hlásilo víc než 22 tisíc lidí z Evropy. Svoboda je astronautem v záloze a teď mu svitla reálná naděje, že se do vesmíru skutečně podívá v rámci některé z misí Axiom. Jeho cestu podporuje vláda i premiér Petr Fiala. Politici jsou nyní nakloněni vesmírnou misi spolufinancovat. Vychází to přit

- undated — Budeme odhalovat exoplanety s pomocí gravitačních vln?
  Budeme odhalovat exoplanety s&nbsp;pomocí gravitačních vln? &#8211; Kosmonautix.cz Přeskočit na obsah Budeme odhalovat exoplanety s&nbsp;pomocí gravitačních vln? Dušan Majer 13. listopadu 2026 0:10 13. 11. 2026 Žádné komentáře 0 5 min čtení Momentálně vyvíjená kosmická observatoř LISA (Laser Interferometer Space Antenna) má detekovat gravitační vlny, tedy drobná zvlnění časoprostoru způsobená obíhajícími hmotami. Nyní se objevily hned dvě studie vedené astrofyziky z&nbsp;NASA, které podrobně ukazují, jak by LISA mohla po celé naší galaxii odhalovat nové a&nbsp;jedinečné populace exoplanet, tedy světů, které leží mimo naší Sluneční soustavu. „ Nejběžnějším zdrojem pozorování pro misi LISA mají být podle očekávání systémy tvořené dvojicí bílých trpaslíků, tedy dvojhvězdy tvořené bílými trpaslíky, kteří obíhají blízko sebe ,“ říká Casey McGrath, postdoktorand na University of Maryland v&nbs

Stejně jako při druhém letu této rakety v&nbsp;roce 2024 došlo k&nbsp;anomálii, kdy odhořela vnější část trysky jednoho z&nbsp;pomocných motorů na tuhé pohonné látky GEM-63XL. Kvůli opakování závady tehdy vojáci dočasně pozastavili své starty na Vulcanu, které budou pokračovat, až se problém vyřeší. Nyní se schyluje k&nbsp;dalšímu startu, který může ukázat, zda se United Launch Alliance podařilo vykročit správným směrem. - undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate
  https://defence24.com/geopolitics/europe-may-be-repeating-its-biggest-mistake-in-space

  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. It was at that point that the Baltic Sea began to be described, in simplified media shorthand, as a so-called »NATO inland sea«. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them. - undated — Manewrowość satelitów kluczowa dla bezpieczeństwa. Liftero chce to wykorzystać [WYWIAD]
  Manewrowość satelitów kluczowa dla bezpieczeństwa. Liftero chce to wykorzystać [WYWIAD] Menu Polska spółka kosmiczna Liftero rozwija chemiczne systemy napędowe dla satelitów, które mają odpowiadać na rosnące potrzeby rynku w zakresie manewrowości i bezpieczeństwa operacji kosmicznych. Technologia BOOSTER została już przetestowana na orbicie podczas misji demonstracyjnej RED5, a firma przygotowuje kolejne systemy dla klientów z Azji. W wywiadzie rozmawiamy m.in. o przewagach napędów chemicznych, perspektywach ich wykorzystania w misjach z zakresu bezpieczeństwa, ekspansji na rynek indyjski oraz planach rozwoju spółki po debiucie na NewConnect. Liftero pozyskało klienta z Azji. Umowa warta blisko 1,5 mln zł Mateusz Mitkow, redaktor naczelny Space24.pl: Na czym polega wyjątkowość technologii rozwijanej przez Liftero i co wyróżnia ją na tle konkurencyjnych rozwiązań? Tomasz Palacz, CEO Lifte

  Europejskie firmy planują konstelację orbitalnych centrów danych Menu OwlO Cloud, niedawno założona luksemburska firma, 12 listopada br. ogłosiła podpisanie dwóch listów intencyjnych (MoU) o współpracy z Thales Alenia Space oraz Edge Aerospace. Partnerstwa te stanowią część szerszego planu OwlO Cloud, zakładającego współpracę z europejskimi producentami satelitów, sprzętu do przetwarzania i przechowywania danych, terminali łączności optycznej oraz sieci przekaźnikowych. Celem jest stworzenie orbitalnej infrastruktury chmurowej, która połączy możliwie jak najwięcej europejskich satelitów i od 2030 r. będzie oferować moc obliczeniową w kosmosie liczoną w megawatach (MW). OwlO Cloud powstała z myślą o wykorzystaniu przez Europę możliwości oferowanych przez skalowalne systemy obliczeniowe na orbicie oraz sztuczną inteligencję (AI). - undated — MON zamawia oprogramowanie do analizy zobrazowań SAR
  MON zamawia oprogramowanie do analizy zobrazowań SAR Menu 12 listopada 2026 r. firma SATIM poinformowała o podpisaniu umowy z Ministerstwem Obrony Narodowej na dostawę oprogramowania do automatycznej analizy zobrazowań satelitarnych pozyskiwanych za pomocą radaru z syntetyczną aperturą (SAR). Postępowanie zakupowe przeprowadziła 2. Regionalna Baza Logistyczna na potrzeby Agencji Rozpoznania Geoprzestrzennego i Usług Satelitarnych (ARGUS) . Z przekazanych informacji wynika, że oprogramowanie będzie wspierać polski program obserwacji Ziemi MikroSAR , pomagając analitykom wydobywać istotne informacje z zobrazowań radarowych. „Satelity SAR mogą obrazować powierzchnię Ziemi w nocy i przez chmury, jednak interpretacja tych zobrazowań bywa trudna i czasochłonna. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje.   Lær om romvirksomhet Til hovedinnhold Lær om romvirksomhet Romvirksomhet omfatter utrolig mye. Det handler om alt fra astronauter, raketter, store romteleskop og romferder til navigasjon og kommunikasjon på jorda og overvåking av klimaet på kloden vår. Satellittnavigasjon Satellittnavigasjon er en teknologi for posisjonsbestemmelse, navigasjon og tidsbestemmelse på land, til sjøs, i luften og i rommet ved hjelp av signaler fra navigasjonssatellitter. Satellittnavigasjon og bruk Satellittnavigasjon er den dominerende teknologien brukt til posisjon, navigasjon og tidsbestemmelse, og brukes blant annet til transport, maritim virksomhet og til effektiv kommunikasjon i luftfart. 5 fakta om satellittnavigasjon Fellesbetegnelsen på satelittnavigasjonssystemer er GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems), men hvordan fungerer det?   https://www.romsenter.no/laer-om-romvirksomhet
- undated — Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører
  Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Til hovedinnhold Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Norge har bygget opp en rominfrastruktur både til kommersiell og offentlig bruk. Norge har vært en romnasjon siden Andøya rakettskytefelt skjøt opp den første forskningsraketten i 1962. Siden har vi bygget opp rominfrastruktur tilpasset landets behov til en hver tid. Vårt medlemskap i ESA og samarbeid med EU har fått stor betydning for dette. Etter småsatellittenes inntog har Norge også bygget opp en flåte av egne nasjonale satellitter som skal dekke behov som vi ikke får dekket gjennom internasjonale samabeid. Nasjonale satellitter AISSat-1 (2010-2022) AISSat-1 var en pionér i Norge og i verden. Eier Direktoratet for romvirksomhet Hovedoppgave Skipsovervåking Dimensjoner 20x20x20 cm. 6 kg. Høyde fra jordoverflaten Ca. 635 km AISSat-1 AISSat-1 i bane (illustrert). Foto: T.

- undated — Slik samarbeider vi med ESA, EU og andre land
  Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Til hovedinnhold Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Bærebjelken i norsk satsing på romvirksomhet er deltakelse i internasjonalt samarbeid. I første rekke gjennom den europeiske romorganisasjonen ESA og EUs romprogram. I tillegg har Norge samarbeidsavtaler med enkeltland. Norge og ESA Den europeiske romorganisasjonen (ESA) er en internasjonal forsknings- og utviklingsorganisasjon grunnlagt i 1975. ESAs formål er å fremme samarbeid mellom europeiske stater innen romforskning og romteknologi for vitenskapelige formål og for utvikling av operasjonsdyktige romanvendelsessystemer. Samarbeid med ESA ESA er den Europeiske romorganisasjonen, og gjennom delegatvirksomheten administrerer Romsenteret Norges deltagelse i ESA.   Вооружённые силы России за неделю нанесли два массированных и 12 групповых ударов по предприятиям военной промышленности Украины, логистическим центрам, объектам энергетической и транспортной инфраструктуры, другим целям противника, сообщает министерство обороны РФ."С 7 по 13 ноября Вооружёнными силами Российской Федерации нанесены два массированных и 12 групповых ударов, в результате которых поражены предприятия военной промышленности Украины, логистические центры, объекты энергетической и транспортной инфраструктуры, использовавшиеся ВСУ, цеха производства и места хранения крылатых ракет и робототехнических комплексов, включая беспилотные летательные аппараты, склады боеприпасов", - сказано в сообщении ведомства, распространённом в пятницу.

Photo: Wikimedia. Senators introduced bipartisan legislation this week aimed at streamlining the application process for new satellites. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3.   https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

  Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial Sep 25, 2026 Destaques GGPEN e Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos assinam Carta de Intenção para promover colaboração em satélites, observação da Terra, capacitação e inovação. O Gabinete de Gestão do Programa Espacial Angolano (GGPEN) e a Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos (UAE Space Agency) assinaram, esta quinta-feira, 25 de Setembro, em Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire, uma Carta de Intenção que estabelece as bases para o reforço da cooperação entre Angola e os Emirados Árabes Unidos no domínio espacial. O documento foi assinado pelo Director-Geral do GGPEN, Dr. Zolana Rui João, e pelo Director-Geral da Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos, Eng. Salem Butti Salem Al Qubaisi, à margem da Africa Space Expo (ASPEX) 2026, onde Angola
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/angola-e-emirados-arabes-unidos-reforcam-cooperacao-no-sector-espacial
- undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate

  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos. - undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection. The key application for this tool is the detection of potential Illegal, Unreported and Unregulated (IUU) fishing activities, to improve sustainable management of m
  https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

  About ZINGSA &#8212; Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency Phone +263 8677009885 Email publicrelations@zingsa.ac.zw Hours Mon&#8211;Fri, 8am&#8211;4pm Contact Us About The ZINGSA story Promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial technologies for Zimbabwe&#8217;s development. Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization. ZIMSAT-1 Zimbabwe&#8217;s first satellite, successfully launched in November 2022. ZIMSAT-2 Zimbabwe&#8217;s second satellite mission, building on the success of ZIMSAT-1 and expanding national capabilities in space technology and Earth observation. CubeSat Development Advanced CubeSat development using 3D printing technology and modern engineering practices to build indigenous small-satellite capability. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html

  Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Home / Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully November 12, 2026 The ground static test of the PSLV third stage (PS3) was carried out successfully at the Static Test Facility in Satish Dhawan Space Centre, Sriharikota on November 12, 2026. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far. The configuration has been strengthened and essential improvements have been carried out in the nozzle configuration to improve design margins along with updated procedures in the acceptance
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Ground_test_of_third_stage_of_PSLV_PS3.html

  ISRO conducts flight acceptance test of Cryogenic engine for next GSLV mission ISRO conducts flight acceptance test of Cryogenic engine for next GSLV mission Home / ISRO conducts flight acceptance test of Cryogenic engine for next GSLV mission November 10, 2026 ISRO successfully conducted the flight acceptance test on November 05, 2026 for the cryogenic engine identified for the next GSLV Mission (GSLV-F19) at the test facility at ISRO Propulsion Complex, Mahendragiri.The ignition of both the Main Engine (ME) and the Gas Generator (GG) occurred as expected. During steady-state operation, the engine performance is normal and consistent with nominal predictions. The test duration was 200 seconds and the performance of the engine was satisfactory and consistent with the pre-test predictions. The engine will be inducted in the Cryogenic stage for the GSLV-F19 mission that will launch the NVS

- undated — Google 研究員稱以 48 個 AI 代理找到未知馬雅遺址，其實 2025 年就發表過
  Google 研究員稱以 48 個 AI 代理找到未知馬雅遺址，其實 2025 年就發表過 | TechNews 科技新報 Google 研究員 Pietro Miotti 宣稱用 AI 代理分析 NASA 光達資料，在墨西哥找到一處可能未記錄過的馬雅遺址，但其實早就刊登於學術期刊，引發考古學界批評業餘人士挾 AI 工具「空降」專業領域。 Google 研究員 Pietro Miotti 本週稍早 宣稱 ，他運用一組 AI 代理在墨西哥找到一處可能尚未測繪的馬雅遺址。但 The Verge 報導，遺址早就發現，2025 年就發表於學術期刊。這起事件在網路引發考古學界批評，突顯 AI 工具日益強大後，業餘人士跨入專業領域卻常缺乏判讀所需專業知識的緊張關係。 Miotti 表示，構想來自他在祕魯旅行時，好奇歷史探險家若擁有現代技術會怎麼做。他選擇光達（LiDAR）這種能產生精細立體地形影像的雷射測繪技術，讓 48 個 AI 代理搜尋 NASA 的光達資料，找尋未記錄遺址的跡象；由於祕魯的公開光達資料涵蓋不足，他轉而聚焦馬雅地區。代理標記多個地點，包括「一處似乎尚未登記的大型聚落」。Miotti 對 The Verge 表示，這是個人業餘專案，沒有使用 Google 運算或其他資源。 Then I asked: which of these don&#8217;t appear anywhere in the literature or the archives? They narrowed it down to one. https://t.co/foloDG4BEe — Pietro Miotti (@PietroMiotti) November 10, 2026 他接著請代理將信件翻成西班牙文，詢問墨西哥國家人類學與歷史研究所（INAH）是否知道此處，對方回覆這位置沒有登記遺址的紀錄。Miotti 在部落格寫道，從旅途一個隨機念頭到收到墨西哥政府正式回覆，只花了不到 72 小時。他強調自己不是考古學家、發現仍需田野調查確認，但仍稱這證明「各門科學都處於革命邊緣」，並呼籲

#### Money Today (press, ko, newsroom)

- undated — AP위성, 다음달 SAR 위성 개발 본격화…"위성 플랫폼 기업으로 도약"
  AP위성, 다음달 SAR 위성 개발 본격화…&quot;위성 플랫폼 기업으로 도약&quot; - 머니투데이 로그인 | 회원가입 닫기 AP위성, 다음달 SAR 위성 개발 본격화…"위성 플랫폼 기업으로 도약" [이 기사에 나온 스타트업에 대한 보다 다양한 기업정보는 유니콘팩토리 빅데이터 플랫폼 ' 데이터랩 '에서 볼 수 있습니다.] 송진환 AP위성 위성사업본부 부사장이 &#039;AP위성 밋업데이 미디어간담회&#039;에서 사업 계획을 설명하고 있다./사진=박기영 기자 " AP위성 (10,180원 ▼240 -2.3%) 은 유닛 단위에서 플랫폼으로 사업을 확장할 계획입니다. 다음달부터는 글로벌 파트너와의 합작법인(JV)을 통해 SAR(합성개구레이더) 위성 개발을 본격화합니다." 송진환 AP위성 위성사업부 부사장은 12일 대전 롯데시티호텔에서 2026 밋업데이 미디어간담회를 열고 향후 사업 구상에 대해 이같이 설명했다. AP위성은 2011년 2월 설립된 회사로 위성통신 단말기와 인공위성 및 부품을 개발·제조한다. 지난 6월에는 400억원 규모 CB(전환사채)를 발행하며 SAR 위성 시장 진출을 발표한 바 있다. AP위성이 새롭게 개발에 나서는 SAR 위성은 레이더를 활용해 지표면 영상을 확보한다. 송 부사장은 "현재 국내에는 SAR 위성을 직접 제조하는 곳이 없으며 전 세계적으로도 경쟁사가 많지 않다"며 "글로벌 파트너와 협력해 SAR 위성 제조 기술과 양산 시설을 확보하고 2028년 1차 발사를 목표로 개발에 나설 것"이라고 말했다. AP위성은 지금까지 특정 부품이나 프로젝트 단위로 진행했던 위성 관련 사업을 전 공정으로 확장해 플랫폼화하고 고객사에 일괄 제공한다는 구상이다. 이 회사는 설립 이후 10여년간 총 25건의 위성 프로펙트에 참여하며 사업 노하우를 쌓았다. 궁극적으로는 최대주주인
